 193: DEVICE_ACCESS(crime)
 194: {
 195: 	/*
 196: 	 *  The CRIME is memory mapped as 0x80 bytes, starting at
 197: 	 *  physical 0x14000000.
 198: 	 *
 199: 	 *  On my real O2:
 200: 	 *
 201: 	 *  0x14000080..0x140000ff is the same as 0x14000000..0x1400007f.
 202: 	 *  0x14000100..0x1400017f is the same as 0x14000000..0x1400007f.
 203: 	 *  0x14000180..0x140001ff is the same as 0x14000000..0x1400007f.
 204: 	 *
 205: 	 *  (I assume that real code will never access the above "mirrors",
 206: 	 *  but it would be trivial to add redirection so that such code
 207: 	 *  would work too.)
 208: 	 *
 209: 	 *  Memory control then starts at 0x14000200.
 210: 	 */
 211: 	struct crime_data *d = (struct crime_data *) extra;
 212: 	uint64_t idata = 0, odata = 0;
 213: 	uint64_t preserved_CRIME_HARDINT = d->reg[CRIME_HARDINT / sizeof(uint64_t)];
 214: 	size_t i;
 215: 
 216: 	if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)
 217: 		idata = memory_readmax64(cpu, data, len);
 218: 
 219: 	/*
 220: 	 *  Set crime version/revision:
 221: 	 *
 222: 	 *  This might not be the most elegant or correct solution, but it
 223: 	 *  seems that the IP32 PROM likes 0x11 for machines without graphics,
 224: 	 *  and 0xa1 for machines with graphics.
 225: 	 *
 226: 	 *  NetBSD 2.0 complains about "unknown" crime for 0x11, but I guess
 227: 	 *  that's something one has to live with.  (TODO?)
 228: 	 */
 229: 	d->reg[CRIME_REV / sizeof(uint64_t)] = d->use_fb? 0xa1 : 0x11;
 230: 
 231: 	d->reg[CRIME_CONTROL / sizeof(uint64_t)] = CRIME_CONTROL_ENDIANESS;
 232: 
 233: 	d->reg[CRIME_INTSTAT / sizeof(uint64_t)] =
 234: 		(d->reg[CRIME_SOFTINT / sizeof(uint64_t)] |
 235: 		d->reg[CRIME_HARDINT / sizeof(uint64_t)]);
 236: 
 237: 	/*
 238: 	 *  Amount of memory.  Bit 8 of bank control set ==> 128MB instead
 239: 	 *  of 32MB per bank (?)
 240: 	 *
 241: 	 *  According to OpenBSD, "Empty banks are reported as duplicates of
 242: 	 *  bank #0."
 243: 	 */
 244: 	if (cpu->machine->physical_ram_in_mb > 1024) {
 245: 		fatal("[ sgi_crime: SGI O2 can not have more than 1024 MB RAM ]\n");
 246: 		exit(1);
 247: 	}
 248: 
 249: 	int mb_per_bank;
 250: 	if (cpu->machine->physical_ram_in_mb <= 256)
 251: 		mb_per_bank = 32;
 252: 	else
 253: 		mb_per_bank = 128;
 254: 
 255: 	if (cpu->machine->physical_ram_in_mb % mb_per_bank) {
 256: 		fatal("[ sgi_crime: for up to 256 MB RAM, RAM size needs to be divisible "
 257: 			"by 32 MB. for larger RAM sizes (up to 1024 MB), it needs to be "
 258: 			"divisible by 128 MB. ]\n");
 259: 		exit(1);
 260: 	}
 261: 
 262: 	int flag_for_128MB = mb_per_bank == 128 ? 0x100 : 0x000;
 263: 	size_t total_mb = 0;
 264: 	for (int bank = 0; bank < 8; ++bank) {
 265: 		int b = mb_per_bank == 128 ? (bank << 2) : bank;
 266: 		if (total_mb >= cpu->machine->physical_ram_in_mb)
 267: 			d->reg[CRIME_MEM_BANK_CTRL0 / sizeof(uint64_t) + bank] =
 268: 				d->reg[CRIME_MEM_BANK_CTRL0 / sizeof(uint64_t) + 0];
 269: 		else
 270: 			d->reg[CRIME_MEM_BANK_CTRL0 / sizeof(uint64_t) + bank] = flag_for_128MB | b;
 271: 
 272: 		total_mb += mb_per_bank;
 273: 	}
 274: 	
 275: 	if (len == 8) {
 276: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)
 277: 			d->reg[relative_addr / 8] = idata;
 278: 		else
 279: 			odata = d->reg[relative_addr / 8];
 280: 	} else if (len == 4) {
 281: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 282: 			if (relative_addr & 4) {
 283: 				d->reg[relative_addr / 8] &= ~0xffffffffULL;
 284: 				d->reg[relative_addr / 8] |= (uint32_t)idata;
 285: 			} else {
 286: 				d->reg[relative_addr / 8] &= 0xffffffffULL;
 287: 				d->reg[relative_addr / 8] |= (uint64_t)(idata << 32ULL);
 288: 			}
 289: 		} else {
 290: 			odata = d->reg[relative_addr / 8];
 291: 			if (relative_addr & 4)
 292: 				odata = (int32_t)odata;
 293: 			else
 294: 				odata = (int32_t)(odata >> 32);
 295: 		}
 296: 	} else {
 297: 		/*
 298: 		 *  #529: THIS USED TO END THE PROCESS.  One guest lb, lbu, lh,
 299: 		 *  lhu, sb or sh anywhere in CRIME did it (witnessed at rung 3
 300: 		 *  on an unmodified -E sgi -e ip32, 6/6).  The width is now
 301: 		 *  DECLINED and announced once, the #498 sgi_de shape: a read
 302: 		 *  is answered 0 through the common tail, a write is dropped.
 303: 		 *
 304: 		 *  The goto skips the register switch below, and that is the
 305: 		 *  point of it: falling through would let a halfword 0x0800
 306: 		 *  written at CRIME_CONTROL stop every CPU, a narrow access to
 307: 		 *  CRIME_REV from the PROM probe loop redirect the PC, one at
 308: 		 *  INTSTAT or INTMASK reassert the interrupt line, and one at
 309: 		 *  CRIME_TIME advance the clock -- all measured.  It is not
 310: 		 *  `return 0' either: that raises a guest bus error
 311: 		 *  (EXCEPTION_DBE, cpus/memory_rw.c), and what real CRIME does
 312: 		 *  on a narrow cycle is not documented here.
 313: 		 */
 314: 		if (!(d->warned & CRIME_W_LEN)) {
 315: 			d->warned |= CRIME_W_LEN;
 316: 			fatal("[ crime: unimplemented len=%i at 0x%x -- declined."
 317: 			    "  (once) ]\n", (int) len, (int) relative_addr);
 318: 		}
 319: 		goto crime_declined;
 320: 	}
 321: 
 322: 	switch (relative_addr) {
 323: 
 324: 	case CRIME_REV:		/*  0x000  */
 325: 		/*
 326: 		 *  A contender for winning a prize for the worst hack
 327: 		 *  in history:  the IP32 PROM probes the CPU caches during
 328: 		 *  bootup, but they are not really emulated, so it fails.
 329: 		 *  During the probe, the CRIME_REV is read a lot. By
 330: 		 *  "returning" from the probe function, i.e. jumping to ra,
 331: 		 *  when this register is read the second time, the probe
 332: 		 *  can be skipped, and the PROM thus runs further.
 333: 		 *
 334: 		 *  The address where this happens is:
 335: 		 *	0xbfc0517c	PROM v2.3
 336: 		 *	0xbfc051ac	PROM v4.13
 337: 		 *
 338: 		 *  4.18 works too. 339: 		 * 353: 	case CRIME_CONTROL:	/*  0x008  */
 354: 		/*  TODO: 64-bit write to CRIME_CONTROL, but some things
 355: 		    (such as NetBSD 1.6.2) write to 0x00c!  */
 356: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 357: 			/*
 358: 			 *  0x200 = watchdog timer (according to NetBSD)
 359: 			 *  0x800 = "reboot" used by the IP32 PROM
 360: 			 */
 361: 			if (idata & CRIME_CONTROL_DOG_ENABLE) {
 362: 				idata &= ~CRIME_CONTROL_DOG_ENABLE;
 363: 			}
 364: 			if (idata & CRIME_CONTROL_HARD_RESET) {
 365: 				int j;
 366: 
 367: 				/*  This is used by the IP32 PROM's
 368: 				    "reboot" command:  */
 369: 				for (j=0; j<cpu->machine->ncpus; j++)
 370: 					cpu->machine->cpus[j]->running = 0;
 371: 
 372: 				idata &= ~CRIME_CONTROL_HARD_RESET;
 373: 			}
 374: 
 375: 			idata &= ~CRIME_CONTROL_ENDIANESS;
 376: 
 377: 			if (idata != 0)
 378: 				fatal("[ CRIME_CONTROL: unimplemented "
 379: 				    "control 0x%016llx ]\n", (long long)idata);
 380: 		}
 381: 		break;
 382: 
 383: 	case CRIME_INTSTAT:	/*  0x010, Current interrupt status  */
 384: 	case CRIME_INTMASK:	/*  0x018, Current interrupt mask  */
 385: 		crime_interrupt_reassert(d);
 386: 		break;
 387: 
 388: 	case CRIME_DOG:		/*  0x030  */
 389: 		// No warning.
 390: 		break;
 391: 
 392: 	case CRIME_TIME:	/*  0x038  */
 393: 		crime_update_crime_time(d);
 394: 		break;
 395: 
 396: 	default:
 397: 		if (writeflag==MEM_READ) {
 398: 			debug("[ crime: read from 0x%x, len=%i:",
 399: 			    (int)relative_addr, len);
 400: 			for (i=0; i<len; i++)
 401: 				debug(" %02x", data[i]);
 402: 			debug(" ]\n");
 403: 		} else {
 404: 			debug("[ crime: write to 0x%x:", (int)relative_addr);
 405: 			for (i=0; i<len; i++)
 406: 				debug(" %02x", data[i]);
 407: 			debug(" (len=%i) ]\n", len);
 408: 		}
 409: 	}
 410: 
 411:  crime_declined:
 412: 	/*  #529: a declined access jumps here, skipping the register switch,
 413: 	    and is still answered.  */
 414: 	d->reg[CRIME_HARDINT / sizeof(uint64_t)] = preserved_CRIME_HARDINT;
 415: 	d->reg[CRIME_INTSTAT / sizeof(uint64_t)] =
 416: 		(d->reg[CRIME_SOFTINT / sizeof(uint64_t)] |
 417: 		d->reg[CRIME_HARDINT / sizeof(uint64_t)]);

 419: 	if (writeflag == MEM_READ)
 420: 		memory_writemax64(cpu, data, len, odata);
 421: 
 422: 	return 1;
 423: }

  11: (witnessed for all six widths at rung 3: regress/artifacts/crimewidthkill/crime_width_witness_20260913.py).  #529 DECLINES the width
  12: with one latched `(once)' announce and a `goto' to the function's common tail, skipping the register switch -- the
  13: #498 sgi_de shape.  A read is answered 0 by the tail, a write is dropped, the PC advances, no exception is raised.
  14: 
  15: Every access here is a real MIPS guest instruction through real address decode and memory_rw, one `step' at a time.
  16: 
  17: CONTRACT -- the edits this detector is designed to catch (clause (e)), each MEASURED in a kill table under
  18: artifacts/crimewidthkill/ (killtable_20260913/ through killtable6_20260913/):
  19:   1. the kill reinstated verbatim, or any other host death at the width arm, before or after the announce
  20:                                                        <op> surv (six widths), hi surv, latch surv, sb08 run
  21:   2. FAULT: the width arm returning 0 (a guest data bus error, EXCEPTION_DBE)   lb/lh/sb/sh pc and exc, lb/lh/lbu/lhu v0
  22:   3. the refused access FALLING INTO THE REGISTER SWITCH -- the goto lost, or narrowed by width, direction, or ADDRESS.
  23:      The switch's observable arms, per refusal cell:
  24:         CRIME_CONTROL (+0x08), writes     sb 0x01 prints the unimplemented-control fatal    sb08 msg
  25:                                           sh 0x0800 stops every CPU (HARD_RESET)            sh08 run, sh08 pc
  26:                                           and the arm's three TESTED idata bits (DOG 0x200, RESET 0x800,
  27:                                           ENDIANESS 0x100) each both ways at 2W: sh 0x0201,
  28:                                           0x0900, 0x0a00 with sh08 0x0800       sh08b msg, sh08b run
  29:                                           plus ALL-ONES refused writes, sb 0xff and sh 0xffff (kimi CLI,
  30:                                           closing pass), so a fall-through keyed on any bit a 1- or 2-byte
  31:                                           store can carry shows (bits above 15 never reach a refused access,
  32:                                           so a conjunct on them is equivalent)  sb08 msg, sh08b msg/run
  33:         and the refused stores at INTSTAT, INTMASK and TIME carry all-ones (0xff / 0xffff) for the same reason
  34:         CRIME_REV (+0x00), reads AND writes, from pc 0xbfc05180: the PROM-probe hack sets pc = ra (it does not test
  35:                                           the direction)                                    lb/lh/sb/sh rev pc
  36:         CRIME_INTMASK (+0x18), CRIME_INTSTAT (+0x10), all four cells: the arm calls crime_interrupt_reassert(),
  37:                                           which raises cause.IP2 after a SOFTINT store   imask/istat lb, sb, lh, sh
  38:         CRIME_TIME (+0x38), all four cells: the arm advances TIME, which the NEXT read shows   time lb, sb, lh, sh
  39:         the default arm (any unlabelled offset): a debug() line, printed while stepping      lb/lh/sb/sh nodflt,
  40:                                           at +0x07, +0x06, +0x1f, +0x1e and +0x27f           hi nodflt
  41:      Each channel carries a LIVE control on an accepted width that shows it can vary:
  42:         rev pc ctl (lw redirects), sw08 halts (sw 0x800 stops the CPUs), sw08 msg ctl (sw 0x01 prints the text),
  43:         imask/istat pre + ctl (IP2 low after the setup; an accepted lw sets it), time lb/sb/lh/sh ctl (an accepted ld
  44:         moves TIME), dflt ctl (lw +0x04 prints the default-arm line).
  45:      The INTSTAT/INTMASK/TIME rows came from the pass-2 Opus seat, which MEASURED a decline narrowed to skip those
  46:      labels passing the 40-row version of this detector 40/40 (opus_pass2_20260913/OPUS_REPORT.md); their 2-byte cells
  47:      from the F2 pass (grok, glm): `len != 2 || relative_addr != CRIME_INTSTAT' passed the 71-row version.  So every
  48:      observable label carries the whole {1,2} x {R,W} refusal floor (CONTROL's reads and DOG have no body: below).
  49:      *** THE sh08 AND sw08 CHANNELS EXIST BECAUSE OF A SEPARATE DEFECT, `crimectllane' (filed): the switch matches
  50:      CRIME_CONTROL by its exact label 0x08 and reads the RAW idata, so a 32-bit write to the high half, or a halfword at
  51:      +0x08, carries bit 11 into HARD_RESET, while NetBSD's +0x0c write does nothing.  A round that fixes that lane
  52:      removes or moves these channels and owes their re-derivation -- the sw08 controls will go red to say so. ***
  53:   4. the latch removed, never set, or KEYED ON THE ACCESS -- the class closed on its single axes after two passes
  54:      found members (F2: `|= len', `|= relative_addr', `<< writeflag'; closing pass: keyed on CRIME_CONTROL, on
  55:      `relative_addr >= 0x40', on address parity).  A keyed latch shows only when the first refusal in a session falls
  56:      where it does not set, so every axis value is a FIRST refusal followed by another, with an announce count:

import ast, contextlib, hashlib, io, pathlib, re
root = pathlib.Path('GXEMUL-SEC')
p = root / 'regress/crime_width_probe.py'
src = p.read_text()
parsed = ast.parse(src)
sessions = []
env = {'re': re, 'BIN': '<trace-only>', 'LABEL': '<trace-only>'}
def capture_session(cmds, **kw):
    sessions.append({'cmds': list(cmds), 'rows': []})
    return '', True
def capture_row(name, got, want, note=''):
    env['rows'].append((name, False))
    sessions[-1]['rows'].append(name)
env.update(session=capture_session, row=capture_row)
body = [n for n in parsed.body if n.lineno >= 173 and not (isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name in ('row', 'session'))]
with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
    exec(compile(ast.Module(body=body, type_ignores=[]), '<command-generation-only>', 'exec'), env)
reverse = {v:k for k,v in env['OPC'].items()}
widths = {'lb':1, 'lbu':1, 'lh':2, 'lhu':2, 'sb':1, 'sh':2, 'lw':4, 'sw':4, 'ld':8, 'sd':8}
for s in sessions:
    regs, mem, pc, events = {}, {}, 0, []

        m = re.fullmatch(r'([a-z0-9]+)=0x([a-f0-9]+)', cmd)
        if m:
            name, val = m.group(1), int(m.group(2),16)
            if name == 'pc': pc = val
            elif name in env['R']: regs[env['R'][name]] = val
            continue
        m = re.fullmatch(r'put w 0x([a-f0-9]+), 0x([a-f0-9]+)', cmd)
        if m:
            mem[int(m.group(1),16)] = int(m.group(2),16)
            continue
        if cmd.startswith('step ') or cmd == '<RUN>':
            for _ in range(int(cmd.split()[1]) if cmd "'!= '"'<RUN>' else 1):
                instr = mem.get(pc,0)
                op = reverse.get(instr >> 26)
                if op:
                    imm = instr & 0xffff
                    imm -= 0x10000 if imm & 0x8000 else 0
                    addr = regs.get((instr >> 21) & 31,0) + imm
                    if env['CRIME'] <= addr < env['CRIME']+0x280:
                        w = widths[op]
                        events.append({'op':op, 'off':addr-env['CRIME'], 'len':w, 'write':op.startswith('s'), 'val':regs.get((instr>>16)&31,0)&((1<<(8*w))-1), 'pc':pc})
                pc += 4
    s['events'] = events

import ast, contextlib, hashlib, io, pathlib, re
root=pathlib.Path('GXEMUL-SEC')
src=(root/'regress/crime_width_probe.py').read_text()
sessions=[]
env={'re':re,'BIN':'<trace-only>','LABEL':'<trace-only>'}
def capture_session(cmds, **kw):
    es=[c for c in cmds if isinstance(c,tuple) and 0xb4000000<=c[1]<0xb4000280]
    if '<RUN>' in cmds: es.append(('lb',0xb4000007,None,0x80001000))
    sessions.append({'events':es,'rows':[]})
    return '',True
def capture_row(name,got,want,note=''):
    env['rows'].append((name,False)); sessions[-1]['rows'].append(name)
def load(A,op,addr,rt,at=None):
    here=A.take(1) if at is None else at
    return [(op,addr,None,here)],here
def store(A,op,addr,val,at=None):
    here=A.take(1) if at is None else at
    return [(op,addr,val,here)],here
env.update(session=capture_session,row=capture_row,ld=load,st=store)

import ast, contextlib, io, pathlib, re
src=pathlib.Path('GXEMUL-SEC/regress/crime_width_probe.py').read_text()
body=[n for n in ast.parse(src).body if n.lineno>=173 and not (isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef) and n.name in ('row','session','ld','st'))]
code=compile(ast.Module(body=body,type_ignores=[]),'<notice-oracle-only>','exec')
widths={'lb':1,'lbu':1,'lh':2,'lhu':2,'sb':1,'sh':2,'lw':4,'sw':4,'ld':8,'sd':8}
def evaluate(predicate):
    gotrows={}; env={'re':re,'BIN':'<trace>','LABEL':'<trace>'}
    def load(A,op,addr,rt,at=None):
        here=A.take(1) if at is None else at
        return [(op,addr)],here
    def store(A,op,addr,val,at=None):
        here=A.take(1) if at is None else at
        return [(op,addr)],here
    def session(cmds,**kw):
        events=[c for c in cmds if isinstance(c,tuple)]
        if '<RUN>' in cmds: events.append(('lb',0xb4000007))
        text='continue\\n' if '<RUN>' in cmds else ''
        warned=False
        for op,addr in events:
            if not (0xb4000000<=addr<0xb4000280 and widths[op]<4): continue
            off=addr-0xb4000000
            if not warned and predicate(off):
                warned=True
                text+='[ crime: unimplemented len=%d at 0x%x -- declined.  (once) ]\\n'%(widths[op],off)
        return text,True
    def row(name,got,want,note=''):
        env['rows'].append((name,got==want)); gotrows[name]=got
    env.update(ld=load,st=store,session=session,row=row)
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()): exec(code,env)
    return gotrows
baseline=evaluate(lambda off:True)
for bit in range(10):
    for required in (0,1):
        candidate=evaluate(lambda off,b=bit,r=required:((off>>b)&1)==r)
        differences=[name for name in baseline if baseline[name]"'!=candidate[name]]
        if not differences: print('"'NO ROW CHANGES: announce only when bit',bit,'=',required)
        elif bit==1: print('announce only when bit',bit,'=',required,'changed rows:',differences)
print('Named-label exact-first-notice checks are absent; counts may move the notice to +0x07 without changing the row.')
'@ | python -B -" in C:\DocumentNoSnc\CC\GXEMUL

announce only when bit 1 = 0 changed rows: ['lb said', 'lh said', 'sb said', 'sh said', 'rearm once', 'top said', 'hi said', 'mid said', 'free said', 'latch x5']
NO ROW CHANGES: announce only when bit 1 = 1
Named-label exact-first-notice checks are absent; counts may move the notice to +0x07 without changing the row.

codex
GAP: in `dev_sgi_ip32.c:91`, replace `CRIME_W_LEN`’s `(1 << 0)` with `(1 << !!(relative_addr & 0x80))`.
`CRIME_CLOSE3_20260913`

The enumeration is incomplete. The independent mutants below are **predicted 121/121**, based on tracing all 33 sessions and their 121 row checks. These are static predictions, not fresh emulator measurements.

1. **The address-bit latch class remains open at bits 7 and 8.**

   The edit above makes the warning latch depend on bit 7. Substituting `0x100` produces the bit-8 counterpart.

   All refusals in the [middle session](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/crime_width_probe.py:695) use `0x1ff`, `0x1fe`, or `0x1fc`; both bits stay set. Every other session’s refusals keep both bits clear. Consequently, neither mutant changes any notice in any session: `mid once` and `mid said` remain 1.

   A refusal crossing either boundary within one device instance would produce a second notice. This directly contradicts CONTRACT 4’s claim that the latch class is “closed on its single axes.” The label-crossing repair was not extended to these address bits.

2. **A notice suppressed whenever address bit 1 is clear also escapes.**

   At [the announcement guard](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_sgi_ip32.c:314), replace:

   ```c
   if (!(d->warned & CRIME_W_LEN)) {
   ```

   with:

   ```c
   if (!(d->warned & CRIME_W_LEN) && (relative_addr & 2)) {
   ```

   Every offset checked by an exact `said` row has bit 1 set. The named labels have it clear, but their counted sessions subsequently refuse at `+0x07`. That later access supplies the missing notice, leaving `rev once`, `sh08b once`, `imask once`, `istat once`, `time once`, and `dog once` at 1.

   Thus, exercising both bit values does not establish announcement coverage. The first refusal’s expected text must be checked in those sessions; counting one notice somewhere permits this substitution.

3. **Branch re-arms are exercised; corresponding branch pre-sets are missing.**

   Insert this single statement inside the existing `CRIME_CONTROL_DOG_ENABLE` branch:

   ```c
   d->warned |= CRIME_W_LEN;
   ```

   The only accepted access entering that branch is `sw 0x0201` in [the re-arm session](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/crime_width_probe.py:628), after the first refusal has already set the latch. The insertion is therefore invisible. The `latch x5` prologue writes `0x01`, which skips the branch.

   The same insertion **inside** the REV PROM-window condition is another survivor. `rev pc ctl` enters it but has no subsequent refusal; `rearm once` enters it after latching. The ordinary REV read added to the latch prologue does not enter that condition. Table 5’s `c2_pre_rev` inserts **before** the condition and therefore does not establish this branch’s coverage.

   There is also an INTSTAT-only pre-set survivor: insert the statement immediately after `case CRIME_INTSTAT:`, before `case CRIME_INTMASK:`. Setup enters INTMASK; accepted INTSTAT accesses occur after refusals.

   The requested enumeration must distinguish **before-first-refusal pre-sets** from **between-refusals re-arms**, for each applicable entry and branch.

4. **Far-block store checks cannot observe a clearing mutation.**

   Replace the width arm’s `goto crime_declined;` with:

   ```c
   if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE && (relative_addr & 0x200))
       d->reg[relative_addr / 8] &= idata;
   goto crime_declined;
   ```

   Every refused far-block store operates on a register containing zero. ANDing it with the incoming data leaves zero, so [hi keeps](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/crime_width_probe.py:675) passes. The separate top session seeds a nonzero register but performs only refused **reads** over it.

   This is a store effect conditioned on one address bit, matching the claimed class. Seed the far register with nonzero data and observe it after each refused store; retain zero-seed coverage for effects that only set bits.

The remaining enumeration also needs precise corrections:

| Claim | Review result |
|---|---|
| Named-label answers | All six labels have a halfword answer check. Repair 7 supplies the advertised nonzero IRQ lanes. CONTROL has no byte-read check; its `0x100` seed has a **zero low byte**. POSTTABLE 5’s “low byte, at both widths” wording is false. This alone does not defeat the width-independent, label-only answer checks. |
| Width, direction, and named-label latch keys | The repaired sequences cross these values. Address bits 7 and 8 lack that transition. |
| Listed branch re-arms | DOG_ENABLE, control text, and REV redirect are interleaved with refusals. This does not establish their pre-set coverage. |
| Every address bit carried both ways by each kind | Literally false: aligned halfword accesses never set bit 0, and `0x1fc` clears bits 0 and 1. More substantively, findings 1, 2, and 4 invalidate the claimed observable coverage. |

The incorrect closure claims are in [CONTRACT 4](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/crime_width_probe.py:53), the [address-bit disclosure](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/crime_width_probe.py:135), and [POSTTABLE 5’s DENOMINATOR](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/crimewidthkill/killtable5_20260913/POSTTABLE.out:43). Exact predictions for selected mutants do not establish those broader classes.

I verified the inlined function/header against the current files, the current probe’s executable AST against frozen table 6, the recorded source hash, and the completion markers in all table-5/6 probe logs. This read-only session could not build or measure new mutants. The outstanding measurements are the seven independent variants above; each has a predicted empty failing-row set.
